SELECT [r].[Id], [r].[CreatedAt], [r].[Description], [r].[IsActive], [r].[Name], [r].[UpdatedAt], [s].[RoleId], [s].[PermissionId], [s].[AssignedAt], [s].[Id], [s].[Action], [s].[Code], [s].[Description], [s].[IsActive], [s].[Module], [s].[Name], [s].[Specificity]
FROM [Roles] AS [r]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [r0].[RoleId], [r0].[PermissionId], [r0].[AssignedAt], [p].[Id], [p].[Action], [p].[Code], [p].[Description], [p].[IsActive], [p].[Module], [p].[Name], [p].[Specificity]
